Evidence must distinguish host fixture from real capture. Exact source/execution/artifact digests and applicable runnable checks required. No shared Gradle contention: Android owns Gradle/integration, API isolated server checks only. Skip formatters/linters/project-wide suites during concurrent edits; run focused owned checks once relevant dependencies ready. Missing source ownership requires PM extension, never mutate accepted sources silently. Final integration requires original lfa-review and lfa-pm exact-revision Gates; device seven checks/thirteen-step closure remains pending. Send START actual full new role todo snapshots parent PM-OP-0004, known parent PM references only, unknown individual mapping null; preserve history/stable IDs. Report Build Trace Verify and real blockers, never infer completion.

Sole writers: lfa-android = Android_App/app/src/test/java/com/example/network/ProductEndToEndTest.kt and herdr-team/.agent-control/EVIDENCE/QR-E2E-01-D01.json, integrated evidence owner; lfa-api = experiments/qr-product-e2e/gateway.py, supplies exact source/execution/artifact digests to Android. This reference does not establish that it is installed. Routine installation must preserve app records with adb install -r; do not uninstall. Runtime endpoint and credentials must come from existing owners through the existing secure configuration flow, never from a guessed address or printed secret.

Seven designated-device checks (ROUNDS/20260918T043217Z-meeting.md:3146): (1) open DHEA page without readable QR and observe disabled shutter; (2) present real 1:QIUQIU:DHEA QR and observe enabled shutter; (3) remove QR and observe invalidation; (4) present a different QR and observe shutter remains disabled; (5) complete capture and confirm a new capture requires re-recognition; (6) confirm complete first-generation JPEG persists and uploads; (7) compare capture and upload SHA. Record actual observations, including failures, without rerunning solely to confirm a user report.

Thirteen-step real-device closure (ROUNDS/20260918T043217Z-meeting.md:3321): (1) designated phone opens DHEA page; (2) shutter disabled before recognition; (3) real 1:QIUQIU:DHEA enables shutter; (4) operator captures complete original JPEG with QR visible in final frame; (5) App persists JPEG/original_sha256; (6) App submits standard API; (7) API persists identical bytes; (8) Core rechecks same product family from final JPEG; (9) query PRODUCT_IDENTIFICATION stage; (10) retain scientific result or explicit domain refusal; (11) observe App server-result display; (12) observe Web product-identification diagnostics for the same request; (13) compare App/API/Core original SHA. Keep original JPEG immutable, capture_bundle_id/request_id/execution_id, timestamps, stage artifacts and actual App/Web observations together. Missing measurements remain null with reasons. A printed/displayed QR is allowed with PRODUCT_FAMILY_TEST_FIXTURE=true, PHYSICAL_DHEA_CARD_ID=null, AUTHENTICITY_CLAIM=false; it does not supply laboratory or measurement validation. All twenty observations remain pending until actual evidence is returned; no host result substitutes for them.

### RUN-20260920-DHEA-MAINLINE-REPLAN-R01 — R04 minimum contract resubmission

This resubmits the R04 contract with a narrower controlled boundary. App owns pre-capture and post-capture single-QR QC. Core input is contractually guaranteed to contain exactly one QR. The controlled physical layout is `C-T-QR`; QR is on the flow-origin side, and the expected 2–3 mm lateral error does not affect direction. Core owns only single-QR geometry reading, association with the existing Window long axis, and deterministic flow-direction determination.

Core explicitly does not perform complete QR perspective correction, well detection, arbitrary-device adaptation, bidirectional scoring, score-tie handling, QR/Window distance thresholds, or a QR conflict matrix. If the single-QR precondition is violated, Core returns an explicit deterministic contract refusal and does not guess direction. Missing/multiple QR are not normal-path Core business-matrix cases. Existing `locate_roi`, rectification, Green extraction, profile, T/C, and guarded research 4PL inversion remain reused.

| exact path | bounded responsibility | writer | status |
|---|---|---|---|
| `core/dhea.py` | replace the dual-direction topology-selection entry point with the single-QR direction contract | lfa-api | PLANNED |
| `core/geometry.py` | single-QR geometry read and deterministic association to the existing Window long axis | lfa-api | PLANNED |
| `core/dhea_diagnostics.py` | selected direction, QR basis, and explicit contract-refusal reason | lfa-api | PLANNED |
| `tests/test_dhea.py` | contract-bound normal direction paths and explicit refusal boundary | lfa-api | PLANNED |
| `tests/test_geometry.py` | single-QR geometry/window-axis association and deterministic direction checks | lfa-api | PLANNED |

Existing lfa-api ownership is retained without expansion: `core/dhea.py` and `core/dhea_diagnostics.py` are already owned under prior tasks; `core/geometry.py` and `tests/test_geometry.py` are already within R01/NODE-01; `tests/test_dhea.py` is the only previously unregistered requested path and remains PLANNED. The prior Review blocking items are narrowed by this product constraint and require a fresh same-version independent Review determination; no prior acceptance is inferred. All five paths remain PLANNED pending that Review and a separate PM gate. No ACTIVE registration, implementation release, source/test edit, runtime/schema/gateway/device/Room/archive change, or arbitrary-device claim is authorized. `IMPLEMENTATION_AUTHORIZED=NO`.
